SCOPE DECLARATIONS IN THE TWO MEMOS\n\nBoth memos declare their scope, and both declare it as the cleanroom only:\n\n- `derived_adopted_open.md:457`: \"READ-ONLY sweep of /Users/bgm/Documents/New project/gravity_emergence_evidence_program/alpha_fundamental_record_action_cleanroom_v003 (848 top-level entries, 458 root *.md, 3,899 files…)\"\n- `derived_adopted_open.md:476` (self-gap 2): \"I did NOT search outside this cleanroom directory; a seal could exist in the parent tree, the supervision lane, or the archive mirror, none of which I was given.\"\n- `live_conditionals.md:504`: \"FILESYSTEM: full corpus listing (848 entries at root)…\"\n\nSo the scope declarations are **honest and explicit**. The failure is not undeclared scope; it is that individual findings are then written in *corpus-wide language* — \"in the entire cleanroom\", \"anywhere in the corpus\", \"anywhere under the program root\", \"NEVER true anywhere\" — and downstream consumers strip the qualifier. `PLAN_TO_ALPHA_V005_ORDERED_2026-07-28.md:42` is the live instance: `REG-4 DCC / MPCP / tau_orth / fidelity k=1 · BRIAN · L · 2 undefined corpus-wide`, with `RD-17 Final-claim sentence · PEND · M · blocked on REG-4` at :53. A Brian-owned decision item and the final-claim sentence are both gated on a negative that is false.\n\nBoth memos also carry a header warning that the `conditionals` refuter FAILED on API errors and that sweep is \"SINGLE-PASS, UNVERIFIED. Treat as leads.\" The live_conditionals memo is therefore self-flagged as unverified — and it is the memo with the most overturned items.\n\n---\n\n## 3. THE FOUR CONSEQUENTIAL RE-SCOPINGS IN DETAIL\n\n### 4.1 QFC — OVERTURNED IN ITS OWN STATED SCOPE (search-term error, not scope error)\n\nThe memo searched for the **acronym**. The **expansion** is a titled, adopted, hash-pinned artifact sitting in the cleanroom root.\n\n- `BID_GLOBAL_BOUNDARY_DESCENT_QUASI_FREE_COMPLETENESS_V001.md:1` — \"# BID Global Boundary Descent and Quasi-Free Completeness v001\"\n- :14 — \"**Global Boundary Descent and Quasi-Free Completeness Principle.** The primitive connected source-record action is exactly the operator-valued functorial CAR lift of the globally assembled one-particle boundary superconnection.\"\n- :22 — \"This is an additional microscopic Gravacle premise. It does not follow from CAR, locality, or disjoint monoidality alone.\"\n- Recomputed sha256 `949181d78aca143ebef06eacc4ab1018d43cdf79962ee8c78c350f654a7555dd` — **MATCHES** the pin at `STAGE8_T7_PRIMITIVE_OPERATOR_RESPONSE_SPEC_V001.md:23`, and the same hash is pinned in `STAGE8_T7_SOURCE_SCALARIZATION_NO_GO_SPEC_V001.md:19`, `STAGE8_T7_CONNECTED_ANALYTIC_CLOSURE_SPEC_V001.md:25`, `STAGE8_T7_GAUSSIAN_PATH_SUM_REDUCTION_SPEC_V001.md:21`, `STAGE8_T7_ACTUAL_PRIMITIVE_CAUSAL_TRANSITION_MAP_SPEC_V001.md:22`, `STAGE6_PARENT_ACTION_LEDGER_SPEC_V001.md:18`, and several provenance manifests.\n- The acronym↔expansion identification is stated outside the cleanroom: `/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha-program-archive/cleanroom_output/14_DCC_QFC_ADJUDICATION_PROTOCOL_V001.md:15-17` — \"**QFC** (Global Boundary Descent and Quasi-Free Completeness, as stated in CURRENT_WORK/source_parent/BID_GLOBAL_BOUNDARY_DESCENT_QUASI_FREE_COMPLETENESS_V001.md and as used by the closure gate): the connected parent is quasi-free (quadratic in CAR fields); quartic terms are excluded.\"\n\n**DCC** is genuinely absent from Roots A and B (Root B's three \"DCC\" hits are base64 blobs in `results/*/certificate.json`), but fully defined in Roots C and D:\n- `/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha-program-archive/cleanroom_output/11_DURABILITY_CLOSURE_PRINCIPLE_V001.md:1` — \"# 11 — Durable-Record Closure Criterion (DCC) v001\"; :27-28 — \"**DC1 (content superselection / phase-only dynamics on facts).** [B, N_i] = 0 for every cell-registration observable N_i.\"; :3 — \"Status: PROPOSED PRINCIPLE — target-value-free, target-aware\"\n- Adjudicated against QFC at `15_DCC_QFC_ADJUDICATION_RESULT_V001.md:38` — \"Both principles **stand, as ADOPTED content, with scopes explicit**\"; :64 — \"*direct conflict* between DCC and QFC is **WITHDRAWN**\"\n- Mirrored in Root D at `_external_handoffs/fable_alpha_cleanroom/OUTPUT/`\n\n**What depended on it and whether it stands.** The memo's conclusion \"kappa_record inherits two premises whose content is not recoverable from this corpus\" (:146) and the register-gap framing at :453 (\"Two register entries (QFC, DCC) have no definition on disk\") do **not** stand. QFC's content is recoverable from the cleanroom; DCC's from Roots C/D, including a sealed adjudication of the QFC/DCC interface. The live consequence is `PLAN_TO_ALPHA_V005_ORDERED_2026-07-28.md:42` `REG-4 … 2 undefined corpus-wide`, a Brian-owned item, with `RD-17 Final-claim sentence … blocked on REG-4` at :53. REG-4 should be re-scoped to \"DCC's definition lives outside the program root and needs importing/pinning\", and QFC struck from it entirely.\n\n### 4.2 Option gamma — NARROWED; the file was on disk two days before the memo\n\n- Memo `live_conditionals.md:366`: \"its TEXT IS NOT PRESENT anywhere under the program root — an 'option gamma' exists on the record whose content this corpus does not carry.\"\n- Memo :511 (self-gap 2): \"`find` over the entire gravity_emergence_evidence_program tree returns nothing for that filename.\"\n- **Fact:** `/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha_supervision/DECISION_2026-07-25_er_fork_option_gamma.md`, mtime **2026-07-25 19:41:08**, recomputed sha256 **`e21cb51ffaaa77caabfbfd4566b7b81053594164a529b49f8cfdca67c9260ab3`** — an exact match to the pin at `STAGE8_T7_RULE7_ORDERING_AUTHORITY_AND_TIMESTAMP_CLAIM_AUDIT_V001.md:160`. A second identical copy at `/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha-program-archive/supervision/`.\n- Two dependent artifacts also exist in Root C: `ROUTE_MEMO_2026-07-25_gamma_record_level_insensitivity.md` (:14 \"Chartered by `DECISION_2026-07-25_er_fork_option_gamma.md` (read first, discipline applied…)\"; :152 \"**Per the principal's rule…: gamma dead, beta**\") and `DISPOSITION_2026-07-25_gamma_killtest_beta.md`.\n- **A sibling memo in the same directory, written 37 minutes later, already had it right:** `PRINCIPAL_REGISTER_2026-07-27.md:885` — \"**Supervision (`/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha_supervision/`)** — `DECISION_2026-07-25_er_fork_option_gamma.md` (his full directive, mandatory discipline, four refutation conditions)…\"\n\n**What depended on it.** The memo's \"DOCUMENT GAP\" framing on the ER-fork item. That gap does not exist. The ER-fork option-(alpha)/(beta)/(gamma) history is fully recoverable, and gamma is already dispositioned \"gamma dead, beta\". This is the cleanest instance of the exact failure mode the audit exists to prevent: a within-lane inconsistency between two memos written 37 minutes apart, caused solely by which roots each searched.\n\n### 4.3 Trotter-Kato / H1 — OVERTURNED BY EVENTS; both memos' headline framing is stale\n\nThe memos were written 2026-07-27 18:02:15. The verdict landed 18:26:10 the same day, in Root C.\n\n- `BOHM_BLIND_TROTTER_KATO_VERDICT_2026-07-27.md:3` — \"**BLINDNESS PROTOCOL.** Six independent agents (3 assigned to prove, 3 to refute)… NO agent had filesystem or corpus access\"\n- :16 \"## 1. THE RULING IS THE PRINCIPAL'S AND NO LANE HAS TAKEN IT.\"\n- `/Users/bgm/MB Work/alpha_supervision/CONTINUATION_STATE.md:729` — \"TRUE — so H1's hypothesis discharges. Full verdict: `BOHM_BLIND_TROTTER_KATO_VERDICT_2026-07-27.md`.\"\n\n**Honest classification:** at the moment of writing, the negative was true in every root. This is supersession by events, not a scoping error — and the memo's self-gap 1 is exemplary: \"I cannot tell from the corpus whether the referral is un-sent, in flight, or returned off-disk. THIS IS THE SINGLE MOST IMPORTANT UNKNOWN AND IT SHOULD BE ANSWERED BY THE PRINCIPAL, NOT BY SEARCH.\" That instruction was correct and was followed.\n\n**But:** the live_conditionals memo is organised around H1 as \"the priority item\" (:9) and around \"The program's next action turns on whether H1 can discharge\" (:21). Its opening 40% is now describing a resolved question. Anyone using it as a standing briefing today will work the wrong item. Note also the still-live caveat at :148-156: H1's target was re-typed onto `|C|_4^{-alpha}·||X||_2`, and \"**Anyone answering the Trotter-Kato referral YES and then declaring R-L2b refuted must first re-type H1 onto |C|_4^{-alpha}||X||_2. The refutation does not transfer automatically.**\" `STAGE8_RL2B_UNIFORMITY_REFUTED_RESULT_V001.md` §1 claims two checks discharging this; I did not verify them and flag it below.\n\n### 4.4 Hostile-audit matrix \"UNSEALED\" — OVERTURNED IN ITS OWN SCOPE by a known-incomplete seal method\n\nThe sweep's seal index was built by walking `*.sha256` files (`derived_adopted_open.md:459`). The hash is pinned in four cleanroom locations that method cannot see, plus one real manifest in Root D:\n\n- `STAGE8_GATE5_KAPPA_RECORD_THEOREM_BATTERY_SPEC_V001.md:95` — \"78f6bb08…cbfe3  BID_CONSOLIDATED_HOSTILE_AUDIT_MATRIX_V005.md\"\n- `STAGE8_GATE5_KAPPA_RECORD_THEOREM_BATTERY_SPEC_AUTHORITY_FABLE_V002.md:66` — same line\n- `results/stage8_authoring_acceptance_v001.json:232,234` — `\"actual\"` and `\"expected\"` both `78f6bb08…cbfe3`, i.e. a **verified acceptance check**\n- `_external_handoffs/fable_alpha_cleanroom/MANIFEST.sha256:52` — \"78f6bb08…cbfe3  ./CURRENT_WORK/source_parent/BID_CONSOLIDATED_HOSTILE_AUDIT_MATRIX_V005.md\"\n\nAnd the lane's **own sibling briefing** names the mode: `BOHM_RULED_STATE_BRIEFING_2026-07-27.md:375` — \"4. **⭐ HASH-PINNED in a governing spec's input table, a results JSON, an audit script or a t_report — as a canonical `<sha256>  <basename>` line inside a `.md` or `.json`, never in a `.sha256` file.** **A walk of `*.sha256` cannot see this**\". So the derived_adopted_open memo's \"three attachment forms, all live\" (:441) is itself incomplete — there are four, and the fourth is what it missed.\n\n**What depended on it.** The instruction at :431 \"Cite it as an audit obligation, not as a sealed ruling\" and the self-gap 2 listing \"TWO UNSEALED LOAD-BEARING ARTIFACTS\". Hostile-audit row A24 — the row the composition-law premise sits under, the arm the corpus actually took — is hash-pinned and acceptance-verified. THE NEGATIVES THAT SURVIVE — THE PROGRAM'S REAL ASSETS\n\n**99 of 223 negatives survive re-running against all four roots.** These are the program's genuine knowledge and should be stated at full strength. The complete list is in `bounded_negatives`; the four that most change what the program can say:\n\n- **`kappa_record` has no value, bound, range, magnitude or asserted rationality in any of the four roots.** The symbol occurs in exactly ONE parent-tree file (a fingerprint JSON). This **settles** the standing UNCHECKED item in `project_exact_rational_observation.md`: the corpus does not supply the rationality that observation would need. *Caveat that matters for the use made of it:* root D DOES compute a magnitude for a differently-named object and bounds a dimensionless ratio to a finite exact set (`21_DIMENSIONLESS_RATIO_RESULT_V001.md:36-37`), converting a continuous underdetermination into a **finite discrete fork**. That family mixes rationals and irrationals, so it neither supports nor refutes the observation — but it was not in scope and someone should look.\n- **No derived stitching/continuum rule exists anywhere in ~36,755 files.** Asserted on 4.8% of the corpus; now rests on all of it, with the two nearest misses affirmatively recording it OPEN. **`project_stitching_rule_absent.md` can be upgraded from a cleanroom claim to a corpus-wide one** — and this is the strongest confirmation in the whole sweep that any finite-cell stiffness route is capped in advance.\n- **Huygens / the R^-1 factor: 17 cleanroom files, ZERO in the parent tree, ZERO in _external_handoffs, zero in all .py in every root.** The tree that holds the induced-gravity coefficient functions contains no Huygens token at all. The instruction \"do not re-search the corpus for the R^-1 factor\" holds corpus-wide.\n- **Stage 8 has never emitted a terminal result**, and the anti-tuning finding is *stronger* than reported: five empty execution directories, not two, and six of seventeen battery obligations never executed.\n\n**A legible pattern worth internalizing:** negatives about a LITERAL SYMBOL the cleanroom owns (`kappa_record`, `kappa_Q`, `D_N`, `F'-5`, `p_effective`, `G_hs`) survive re-scoping almost without exception. Negatives about a CONCEPT the wider program has worked on under other names (gauge orbit, ghosts, matching, renormalization condition, transversality, elimination, consistency condition, overdetermination, anomaly cancellation, induced coefficients) almost never do. **That is the rule for deciding which negatives are safe to cite.**\n\n**One counter-warning against mechanical re-running:** `D_N` returns 42 lines in the parent tree, and every one is a **different object** (a Dirac direct-sum counterfamily). A naive corpus-wide re-run would produce 42 false positives. Scope-widening is necessary and not sufficient; the type must be checked.\n\n---\n\n## §4. COUNT SUMMARY\n\n**14 memos. 224 rows (223 bounded negatives + 1 measurement). ~186 distinct objects after dedup** (≈37 rows are the same object audited by two or more agents — Trotter-Kato ×4, BRIDGE_ABSENT ×3, namespace register ×3, verify_v002 ×3, tail certificates ×3, and ~22 pairs; the dedup is approximate and I say so).\n\n| Verdict | Count | % of 223 |\n|---|---|---|\n| **SURVIVES_CORPUS_WIDE** | **99** | 44.4% |\n| **NARROWED** | **51** | 22.9% |\n| **OVERTURNED** | **39** | 17.5% |\n| **UNVERIFIABLE_BY_SEARCH** | **25** | 11.2% |\n| NOT_RERUN (reason stated) | 8 | 3.6% |\n| CLASS VERDICT (covers ~35 further flags) | 1 | — |\n\n**90 of 223 negatives — 40.4% — do not stand as written.**\n\nOf the 39 OVERTURNED: **19 are overturned in the memo's own declared scope** (in-scope under-search, hyphenation/inflection misses, label-vs-content misses, seal-mode undercounts) and **20 are overturned by scope or by events**. That split matters: **widening the scope would have caught only half of them.** The other half needed a second search term, a second spelling, or a second read of a file already in scope.\n\nOf the 51 NARROWED: essentially all were found by running the memo's own stated regex against the three roots it did not search. No cleverness was required, only scope.\n\n---\n\n## §5. METHOD FINDING\n\n### 5.1 What fraction declared their scope\n\n| | Memos | Fraction |\n|---|---|---|\n| Declared a search root explicitly and honestly | 10 | **71%** |\n| Declared NO scope anywhere | 3 | 21% |\n| Declared the *opposite* of a scope (\"No corpus access\") and then asserted a corpus-wide grep result | 1 | 7% |\n| **Declared scope INSIDE each negative's own sentence** | **1** | **7%** |\n\nThe three that declared nothing: `BOHM_CONSISTENCY_CONDITIONS_SWEEP_2026-07-28.md`, `BOHM_DIVERGENCE_RETYPING_SWEEP_2026-07-27.md`, `BOHM_SWEEP_2026-07-27_route_graveyard.md`. Note which they are: the memo carrying the week's central negative, and the memo titled \"*every route/mechanism/lever ever proposed*\". **The two highest-claim artifacts are the two with no root declaration.**\n\n### 5.2 The finding that actually matters: header-level scope has ZERO protective effect\n\n**71% declared their root — and produced 39 overturns and 51 narrowings anyway.** Declaring scope in a header or in a trailing §GAPS block does not work, for a mechanical reason: these documents are *designed* to be consumed one entry at a time. A lane reads one briefing row. It never sees the qualifier 500 lines away. Meanwhile the body language is universal — one agent counted **40 unqualified \"corpus-wide\"/\"anywhere\"/\"nowhere\" phrases across two memos** whose scope was one directory holding 537 of the program's ~4,100 non-vendored markdown files.\n\nThe contrast is clean and it is the single most useful number in this report:\n\n- **`BOHM_CLEANROOM_SCOPE_PROVENANCE_2026-07-28.md`** is the only memo that put the exact search and the exact target *inside each negative*. Its section is headed `:347` \"### BOUNDED NEGATIVES (each is exactly the search named, nothing wider)\". **All 14 of its negatives survive re-verification. 14/14.**\n- **`BOHM_SWEEP_2026-07-27_traps.md:800`** is the only *individual* negative in all 14 memos that names its four trees in its own sentence. **It survives.**\n- Everything else: 44% survival.\n\n### 5.3 The discipline that would have prevented this class\n\n**THE RULE.** No bounded negative may be recorded without all five of these, *in the negative's own sentence*:\n1. **the literal pattern**, as run;\n2. **the enumerated root paths**, all of them;\n3. **the enumerated exclusions**, by name (the program has no canonical vendored list — this is why five agents got a 2.2× spread on the same root);\n4. **hyphen / space / inflection variants** of the pattern (`overdetermin` missed `over-determined` *inside its own scope*; `superselection sector` returned 0 while `superselection` returned 19 cleanroom files);\n5. **a type check**: if the negative is about a CONCEPT rather than a token, it must be labelled UNVERIFIABLE_BY_SEARCH and may not be cited as a search result.\n\n**FOUR SUPPLEMENTARY RULES, each earned by a specific failure above:**\n- **A negative about a LABEL is not a negative about the OBJECT.** (QFC's acronym vs. its expansion; the R-16 \"no row\" inference; the ~35 register flags.) The corpus has already adopted this rule for the register at `STAGE8_LANE_STATUS.md:270-273` — generalize it.\n- **Before declaring a gate unrun, check for its RESULT — and check the reverse direction first.** A sealed script hash in a battery spec implies a run happened *somewhere*. Two of the five scripts listed at `STAGE8_GATE5_…:114-139` had results nobody looked for.\n- **Test all four seal-attachment modes, in all four roots.** Seven wrong \"UNSEALED\" findings and counting. Mode 4 — a `<sha256>  <basename>` line inside a `.md` or `.json` — cannot be seen by a walk of `*.sha256`, and the parent tree's *execution provenance* is where mode-4 pins for cleanroom files actually live.\n- **Date-stamp every state claim and give every memo an expiry marker.** Five negatives were true when sealed at 18:00–18:02 on 2026-07-27 and false within hours. These memos are written in the present tense and carry no expiry. Any consumer must date-check against `STAGE8_LANE_STATUS.md` as it stands.\n\n**AND THE ONE STRUCTURAL RULE, from §0:** a corpus-wide negative may not be certified by a single agent. An agent briefed specifically to catch this error, with all four roots in its instructions, still certified \"zero hits anywhere\" after searching one root. **Independent re-derivation, not a second pair of eyes on the same output.**\n\n---\n\n## §6. NEGATIVES THAT CANNOT BE SETTLED BY SEARCH — 25, DECLARED AND NOT FAKED\n\nGrep can confirm a vocabulary is absent. It cannot confirm a *thing* is absent. These 25 are concept-shaped and I decline to manufacture verdicts for them. They must never be cited as search results, and several are already correctly typed by the corpus itself.\n\n**The load-bearing ones:** \"no condition constrains a magnitude\"; \"the corpus has none of the four\" (horizon / entropy functional / monotonicity / extremization); \"every condition in this corpus is preserved by adding a small positive c_R\"; \"the condition class is closed under the deformation it would have to exclude\"; `BRIDGE_ABSENT` as a concept (\"no artifact *defines an extraction*\"); \"no artifact connects X to the Galerkin requirement list\"; \"sealed text is silent in both directions\"; \"no sealed artifact *supplies grounds for*…\"; \"there is no third numerator anywhere\"; \"'environmental' has no Hilbert space, coupling or tracing map\"; \"no such target-independent boundary CFT has been derived\"; \"the corpus appears to deny it\"; the Dixmier verdict's four mathematical claims (X2–X4 and the d=3 no-log table).\n\n**Two are unreconstructible rather than concept-shaped** and should simply be dropped or re-stated: \"0 hits under 11 phrasings\" (the 11 are never listed) and \"zero under every phrasing\" (unenumerated). **Any negative resting on N unnamed phrasings is not auditable. Name them or drop the count.**\n\n**Three deserve honest credit for typing themselves correctly:** `live_conditionals:205` (\"a BOUNDED NEGATIVE EXISTENTIAL… IT IS NOT A THEOREM\"), `live_conditionals:233` (silence in both directions typed OPEN), and `BOHM_CONSISTENCY_CONDITIONS_SWEEP:142` (flagged \"as analysis and not corpus text\"). The failure is not that these were written — it is that nothing prevents a downstream lane from citing them as if they were searches.\n\n---\n\n## §7. HONEST LIMITS OF THIS SYNTHESIS\n\n1. **I verified seven items myself; the rest I carry from six agents.** Given that one of those agents was demonstrably wrong on a claim in its own report (§0), a residual error rate in the un-verified rows should be assumed. The seven I ran: the superconnection digest (19 pins), `consistency condition` (0 / 66), the DCC chain's existence and its zero supervision references, `CALIBRATION_LEDGER.md`, the route ledger (796 lines), the Gate-4 script digest (exact match across cleanroom spec and root-D chain), and `CONTINUATION_STATE.md:755-756` with its two counterexample counts (Sakharov 4, induced coupling 5).\n2. **The deduplicated count (~186) is approximate.** The raw row count (224) is exact.\n3. **Root B was searched by STRING, not by CONCEPT, by every agent.** One agent measured the trees as lexically **disjoint on Stage-8 labels and heavily overlapping on physics concepts** — `R-L2b`, `kappa_record`, `Trotter`, `zero-free`, `linked cluster` all return 0 in root B, while `Hessian` returns 298, `cumulant` 189, `stiffness` 173, `Sakharov` 27. **A `SURVIVES_CORPUS_WIDE` verdict earned by running a Stage-8-vocabulary term against root B is close to vacuous with respect to the parent tree.** Do not let anyone convert those zero-hit results into \"the parent tree has nothing on this.\" This is where the next inversion will come from.\n4. **The ~35 \"NOT IN REGISTER\" flags were condemned as a class from a sample of three, not enumerated.** So were the briefing's 16 \"genuinely unsealed\" files (3 of 16 tested, all 3 wrong).\n5. **Not adjudicated, and flagged for the principal:** whether \"R-L2b's uniformity is refuted\" satisfies the R-16 trigger \"R-L2b FAILS\"; whether the refutation transfers after re-typing H1 onto `|C|_4^{-alpha}·||X||_2`; whether the DCC chain's DC1 is violated by adding a small positive `c_R`; and whether the parent tree's matched-regulator CTP derivations satisfy the seven Admissible Galerkin conditions (BN-T19 — nobody has checked, and it is worth someone's time).\n6. **Not re-run at all:** `review_packets/` and `tests/` inside the cleanroom were in the file lists but not read; the target-token workspace scan was deliberately not re-run (alpha firewall); and `CURRENT_AUTHORITY_LEDGER_V010.json` plus three parent audits needed to settle the two stiffness-route revival triggers (B-18) were not opened. **B-18 is the highest remaining scope risk: finite-cell stiffness routes are exactly the material the parent tree carries, and no memo in this set ever searched it.**\n7. **A second live authority chain outside the cleanroom was searched by none of the 14 memos:** `alpha_post_cleanroom_current_authority_spec_v001.json`, carrying seven `active_physics_inputs`, a 14-key `claim_state` (all false, including `\"coupling_computed\": false`), and `\"step_5_derivation_work_authorized\": true`. **Any future negative phrased \"corpus-wide\" that does not read this file is unscoped by construction.**\n8. **Parent-tree material is live, not archival.** `AUTHORITY_CHAIN_PRECEDENCE_PRINCIPAL_DECISION_2026-07-29.md:33-37`: \"*NOTHING IN THE PARENT TREE IS RETIRED, REFUTED, OR DISCARDED BY THIS RULING… all remain live evidence*\", and `:23` makes it \"*ADMISSIBLE AS EVIDENCE AND AS INPUT — not as procedure.*\" So every NARROWED item above is a **live-evidence gap**, not a housekeeping note — and none of these findings disturbs the cleanroom's procedural authority.\n9. **No alpha, kappa_record, kappa_Thomson, g_N, λ_c, c_R, coupling, root, depth or scale was computed or evaluated.** The strings `g_N·T_R = πħ`, `{1/16, √2/16, 1/8, 1/4, √2/4, 1/2}`, `H_handle = (1/16)[…]`, `2/pi`, `S(ell_*) = 0` and `c = 1/4` appear above **only** as verbatim transcriptions of corpus text, quoted to establish that an artifact exists and what type of object it is — never used, compared, or reasoned toward."
}
